### 3. The strategies one by one

"Inherent" follows from how the strategy works. "Spike bug" is a defect of this spike's code that a
fix would remove. "Engine or product" shows in every strategy, the frame's unmodified product mount
included.

#### naive

| Aspect                   | Finding                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| ------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| How it works             | The host stays in the node at the node body's size and the board's CSS transform scales it. The canvas is never told the board zoom.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| Right                    | At zoom 1: 27 of 27 gestures. At any zoom: click and hover targets, the label editor's box (within 0.7 px), typing, Delete, the FEEL caret (CodeMirror detects the scale itself), Wardley's evolve handle (0.98 to 1.00).                                                                                                                                                 |
| Wrong, inherent          | Every pointer conversion is off by the board zoom: move ratio 0.51 / 0.72 / 1.50 / 2.01, a created task 166 px left of the pointer at 0.5 and 1312 px right of it at 2, a column width of 150 stored where 272 is right. Fixed boxes are off by the node's client origin, (60, 80) px, at zoom 1 too. Context Maps edits the name for the team and the team for the name. |
| Wrong, engine or product | Section 7, and worse for the no-change commit: an annotation's label editor closed without typing resizes it by the zoom again (a stored value moved 27 units at 0.5 and 211.8 at 2).                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| Cost                     | As the other in-page strategies (473 KB of heap per editor).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |

#### counter

| Aspect                   | Finding                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| ------------------------ | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| How it works             | The armed host is laid out at node size times board zoom and carries `scale(1 / zoom)`: net scale 1 under a transformed ancestor. A view controller owns the node's logical view and writes the viewbox again on every board zoom step; the write is held while a text field of the editor has the focus or a shape is held. diagram-js's popup menu and tooltip are created in a body-level host, Context Maps' fixed prompt is shifted back by CSS.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| Right                    | Pointer-exact in every engine: bpmn-js 133 of 135 gesture rows ok, no wrong-data row anywhere. Popup menus at their anchor within 2 px at every zoom and both node sizes. Equal to `overlay` in verdict in every battery row but 6.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| Wrong, inherent          | Chrome and HTML views keep their screen size. At 0.5 the palette (94 x 335 px) loses 4 of 14 entries in the 800 x 508 node and 6 of 14 in the 640 x 388 node, and the decision table is 0 % visible (9 % at 0.73, 41 % at 1): at 0.5 the DRD's drill-down button takes no press, so the table cannot even be opened by pointer, and at 0.73 it opens and no body cell takes a press, also after it is scrolled to its end (0 of 25 cells, 3 of 3 rows each, section 10). The view is written again per zoom step: with an editor open or a shape held the picture is 56.5 % out of scale at 39 of 40 steps, the DRD, Wardley and Context Map popups close at step 1, and a shape dropped at 2.3 times the zoom of its press ends 331 to 698 px from the pointer. Arming at 0.8137 re-lays label text (0.62 to 1.14 % of the pixels, 10.1 % for the table). At board zoom 0.25 no DRD or Context Map shape can be pressed, at 0.1 none of three notations. |
| Wrong, spike bug         | Context Maps' fixed prompt stays in the transformed host: 24 % visible at 0.5, 80 % at 0.73, and for a context at the edge the node body scrolls by 437 / 329 / 240 / 160 flow px (the 6 rows that differ from `overlay`). The editor's own zoom was not kept inside diagram-js's range of 0.2 to 4 (wheel-limits degraded in 5 of 5 rows, as in `overlay`; fixed in round 5, section 8). At 0.8137 the decision table stands 10 units lower after disarming (not investigated).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| Wrong, engine or product | Section 7. The no-change commit of a label editor happens at the fitted scale at every zoom (annotation 1.48 to 5.92 units wider) and at editor zoom 100 % at 0.73 and 1.37.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| Leans on                 | The private `popupMenu._createContainer` and `hoverTooltip._mount` for the re-parenting, the class names of the editor's containers mirrored above the popup, and the view controller's rule for when a view write is held.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| After an engine update   | Without the re-parenting the tooltip is off by exactly the host's client origin, (60, 96) to (60, 128) px, and the replace menu's entry could not be hit at 0.73 (measured with the override removed).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| Cost                     | Memory as in-page. With a node armed, one wheel step of the board costs 2.09 ms of layout (0.23 to 0.27 in `naive`, `iframe`, `plugin`, `shim`) and 10.21 ms of main thread (7.29 to 7.95); 20 viewbox writes per 20 steps.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |

#### overlay

| Aspect                   | Finding                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| ------------------------ | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| How it works             | While armed, the same host element is moved into a layer above the board and placed from the node's flow rectangle and the viewport on every React Flow store update, snapped to device px; on disarm it goes back into the node. The view controller is `counter`'s. No engine patch.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| Right                    | Pointer-exact with no engine patch at all. Popups, the fixed Context Maps prompt (0 px, 100 % visible) and tooltips are right unpatched and free to reach beyond the node. The host is within 0.6 px of the node body and follows a pan, a header drag and a resize in the same frame.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| Wrong, inherent          | Everything `counter` has from screen-size chrome and from the re-applied view. In addition the editor lies above the whole board: a note dragged across passes under it, a note or image dropped on the armed node stays hidden until disarm, a fifth to a quarter of the two bottom corner resize handles is covered (37 of 49 and 40 of 49 px take a press), and Tab leaves the node (to the Files panel).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| Wrong, spike bug         | An image pasted after a label edit arrives on a div of the editor and is dropped. Fixed in round 5 (section 8): Backspace after a press on a non-focusable part of the armed editor deleted another selected board node, and the wheel range was unclamped as in `counter`. Found in round 5 (section 12): the Done button out of reach for a node wider than the board, and the selection lost on every disarm. Round 6 (section 13): a Done control in the toolbar is in reach wherever the node lies (339 of 339 and 504 of 504 presses) and the selection is still lost; found: a drag released over a switched-off toolbar button is never ended (9 of 9 pages), and the overlay layer scrolls 15 to 16 px under a label editor that crosses the board's right edge (3 of 3 in four settings). |
| Wrong, engine or product | Section 7, with `counter`'s numbers for the no-change commit.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| Leans on                 | React Flow's store (subscribed directly, to follow a move or resize of the node) and one stacking rule: a popup that reached 203 px over the Files panel was covered there until the board's box got `z-index: 1`. No engine private member.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| After an engine update   | Nothing in the engine is touched. A later positioned sibling of the board can cover a popup again.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| Cost                     | Memory as in-page. With a node armed: 1.76 ms of layout per wheel step, and a board pan costs 7.89 ms of main thread per move at 20 nodes where the others need 6.20 to 6.67.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |

#### iframe

| Aspect                   | Finding                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| ------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| How it works             | A same-origin frame per node, sized in flow units and scaled by the board. Its page mounts one product editor with the product's own mount function on a replica that syncs with the hub over `postMessage`. The editor has a document and a window of its own.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| Right                    | Exact by construction: every displacement in frame px is the same number at every zoom. No broken or wrong-data row of its own in the six editor batteries. HTML views scale and stay editable (41 % of the table at every zoom, column widths 272 / 247 / 232 / 219 / 212 for a 40 px drag). Edits at board zoom 0.1, 0.25, 3 and 4 (16 of 16 rows). Keys and wheel never leave the frame (0 keydown events in the board page).                                                                                                |
| Wrong, inherent          | Popups are confined to the frame's window: in the 800 x 508 node the replace menu never opens at its anchor, in a 640 x 388 node 17 % of it is cut for an element at the right edge and 28 % of the colour menu for one at the bottom; Wardley's 358 px settings menu opens 136 frame px from the click. Chrome scales (pad entries 11 px, table text 7 px at 0.5) and one wheel step scrolls 37.5 to 150 px. Simultaneous edits become crossed writes (`postMessage` is asynchronous): which one survives varies between runs. |
| Wrong, spike bug         | While the frame has the focus, Shift + drag on the bare board pans it by the whole drag (2466.8 px) instead of selecting, and an image file dropped on the armed editor is lost (no hint, no node, no file). Both have a fix on the board side that was not built.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| Wrong, engine or product | The product's first fit in a small frame: the DRD's drill-down button lies under the definitions card (20 % of it takes a press: 10 of the 11 degraded DMN rows), a start event under the palette at every zoom, the pool's resize handle inside the 20 px auto-scroll zone (5 of 5 rows). No reaction to a resize of the frame.                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| Leans on                 | Nothing in an engine. The host owns three pieces of glue: two focus guards (`inert` does not stop a `focus()` made inside the frame), a pointer shield while the board page has a press (before it a resize got 2 of 11 mouse moves), and "ready" and "release" read off the DOM because the mount contract has neither.                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| After an engine update   | Only the two DOM heuristics can break (`[data-element-id] > .djs-visual` for ready, a synthetic click on `.djs-container > svg` for release). An import-done signal, a `release()` and a resize reaction in the mount contract would remove them.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| Cost                     | Heap 1486 KB per editor against 473 KB (3.1 times; 2.8 times without the test tool's 164 KB per frame). At 30 editors 49.3 MB of heap against 20.2 MB and 199 to 224 MB of anonymous resident memory against 89.5 to 90.1 MB. Mount 67.5 ms per further editor against 19.6 to 21.9 (one run). 42 requests per frame, answered from the cache. A node drag costs 7700 us of compositing CPU per move against 650 to 800 (software compositing).                                                                                 |

#### plugin

| Aspect                   | Finding                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| ------------------------ | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| How it works             | Placement as `naive`. One didi module per editor reads the outer scale s (the container's client width over its layout width) at every use and repairs nine sites through public events, service overrides and members set on that one editor's services. `&plugin=` switches its layers.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| Right                    | bpmn-js: 135 of 135 gesture rows ok, 175 of 182 chrome rows. The DRD is exact at five zooms and equal to `overlay`. No view is written on a board zoom: 0 of 40 steps out of scale with an editor, a popup or a held shape, and a shape dropped at 2.3 times the zoom of its press rests 0.8 to 1.3 px from the pointer. Closing a label editor without typing never writes (0 of 36 closes; the only strategy). Chrome is never clipped (64 of 64 pad entries and 14 of 14 palette entries take a press at every zoom). Arming changes 0 % of the pixels.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| Wrong, inherent          | What the module does not reach behaves like `naive`. Decision table: the lists of `InputSelect` (hit policy list (582, 502) px off at zoom 2 with 0 of 11 entries in reach; a type can only be typed at 1.5 and 2) and the context menu's own clamp (at 0.5 "Add rule below" and "Add input right" are out of reach). The literal expression view gets no module. The label editor's resize handle stores a wrong width (+32.5 and +121.3 units at 0.5 and 2 for a travel of 59.1). 20 renderer-own sites (section 4). Chrome scales with the board. Popups belong to the scaled host: they reach beyond the node and not beyond the board's box, which cuts them (node 10 px from the board's right edge at zoom 1: 42.9 % of the replace menu is in reach and 0 of 8 entries take a press at their middle; `shim` flips the same menu to the left and shows all of it, `overlay` lays 58.5 % of it over the Files panel, section 10). |
| Wrong, spike bug         | A menu that reaches beyond the board's right edge is placed a second time while the browser scrolls for the focused search field: 145 px off, 47 % visible (zoom 2, 7 of 7 measurements). Column widths are saved unrounded (246.7945255645334; a click rewrites 192 as 192.00000501658818 at 0.73). Auto-scroll's 20 px thresholds are not scaled: at board zoom 0.1 a DRD drag of (12, 8) px moved the shape (12, 62) px. Two renderer paths hand the popup layer layout px, which it converts as client px (Wardley's keyboard pad menu 302 px off at 0.5, 44 px in `naive`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| Wrong, engine or product | Section 7, except the no-change commit: the layer `edit` removes it, at scale 1 too, which is a change of the engine's behaviour beyond the outer scale.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| Leans on                 | Private members of diagram-js and its companions: `_getTargetBounds`, `_ensureVisible`, `directEditing.$textbox`, `_active.context.bounds`, the field names of the drag context, closures that read the canvas only through `viewbox()`, `scroll()` and `_container`, a copy of 25 lines of dmn-js-decision-table, and for `own` service and method names read out of the renderers' built bundles.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| After an engine update   | Every patch is guarded by `typeof member === "function"`: a renamed member makes that site behave like `naive` again, silently. Only a measurement notices.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| Cost                     | Memory as in-page (474 KB of heap per editor). 0 viewbox writes and 0.25 ms of layout per wheel step. Script per pointer move 354 us (302 to 349 in the four older strategies).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |

#### shim

| Aspect                   | Finding                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| ------------------------ | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| How it works             | Placement as `naive`. On arming, a hidden probe finds origin O and scale s of the host's fixed containing block; a true point p is the virtual point (p - O) / s. 25 capture listeners on `window` give the editor's pointer events virtual coordinates, and 13 patched functions and getters (client rectangles, point queries, the screen matrix, the window's size) answer in that space. Disarming puts the originals back.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| Right                    | No broken and no wrong-data row of its own: bpmn-js gestures 135 of 135, chrome 166 of 182, DMN 124 of 125 (the lists at 0 / 0 px, column widths whole numbers), Miragon 662 ok, 48 degraded and 15 unmeasured of 725. Every box a renderer places from client rectangles is within about 1 px, the fixed Context Maps prompt included. Nothing is written on a board pan or zoom (0 of 40 steps out of scale). Edits at board zoom 0.1 to 4 (16 of 16 rows).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| Wrong, inherent          | The editor gets whole virtual px, so above zoom 1 its pointer has a grain of `zoom` screen px: an Event Storming sticky dropped from the pad lands 2.2 px off at 1.5 and 2 (rule 2 px), a connect preview up to 2.98 px. Chrome scales with the board, one wheel step scrolls 37.5 to 150 px. Editor code that asks for the window's size or passes a point from a timer or animation frame, before it read geometry of its own, gets the true answer (0 of 13 right). Every script on the page sees virtual rectangles for nodes inside the armed host, and a job that read one of them gets virtual answers for the rest of that job: `innerWidth` 2465.75 for 2200, the board's rectangle at (-82, -110) for (0, 0), another element from `elementFromPoint` (zoom 0.73, 6 of 6 cycles, section 10). From any timer or animation frame the document element's rectangle is the editor's window (2465.75 x 1671.23 for 2200 x 1300). Board code that measures a node's content and then anything else in the same job is affected while a node is armed. |
| Wrong, spike bug         | `documentElement.clientWidth` and `clientHeight` are not patched: the FEEL autocomplete list is parked at top -10000 px when the cell's layout offset in the node exceeds the window's client size (window 2200 x 440 at zoom 0.5, 3 of 3 runs; `shim=all,guess` does not help; with both answered in the editor's space the list is back; a second, independent probe finds the same threshold: a cell at layout offset 425 px shows its list in a 440 and a 420 px high window and not in a 400 or 360 px one, 4 of 4 rows, where `plugin` shows it in all five). In a shim node CodeMirror's tooltips are positioned absolutely and the table's scroll box cuts the list to 11.4 of 71 px (cause not found). A modal dialog inside the host is reported in the wrong space (synthetic only).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| Wrong, engine or product | The no-change commit of a label editor at the node's fitted scale, exactly as the unscaled editor writes it (annotation 2.96 units wider at all six zooms).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| Leans on                 | No engine private member. Browser behaviour instead: own properties on event objects shadowing the prototype getters, `window.event`, Chromium's 1/64 px layout grid (direct editing compares by `!==`), whole px in mouse events, configurable accessors on `window`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| After an engine update   | Nothing fails loudly. A measurement that moves into a timer, an observer or past an `await` flips from right to wrong without an error, and a geometry API not on the list (`movementX`, `getCoalescedEvents`, `getBoxQuads`, the top layer) is a silent gap. Every regression is a wrong number that only a battery finds.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| Cost                     | Memory as in-page (473 KB of heap per editor). Script per pointer move 399 us where the others need 302 to 354. An armed editor adds nothing measurable to board work beside it (3820 against 3774 us per move).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |

### 4. Can one diagram-js plugin solve it?

For diagram-js itself: yes. Nine sites (seven in diagram-js, two in diagram-js-direct-editing) are
repaired by one module of about 700 lines without patching a prototype, and the batteries confirm
it for bpmn-js and the DRD. For the Miragon renderers: no. They compute pointer positions and place
boxes in code of their own that no diagram-js service leads to. For the decision table: no. It is
component code (table-js, dmn-js-shared) without services.

| Site in diagram-js 15.28.1 and direct editing 3.6.0     | What was wrong                                                                         | Mechanism                                                                           | Private members it leans on                                      | Result with the module                                                      |
| ------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| 1 `Dragging` `toLocalPoint` (every drag)                | Client offset divided by the viewbox scale only                                        | Public events `drag.init`, `drag.move` at priority 100000 and `dragging.context()`  | Field names of the drag context                                  | Move 0.99 to 1.007 in seven engines (`overlay` 0.99 to 1.008)               |
| 2 `MoveCanvas` `handleMove`                             | Pointer delta in client px handed to `canvas.scroll()`                                 | Service override: a subclass on a canvas facade                                     | None by name; that it reads the canvas through `scroll()` only   | Pan 1.00                                                                    |
| 3 `ZoomScroll` `_handleWheel`                           | Pivot and scroll distance in client px                                                 | Service override of the public `zoom()` and `scroll()`                              | None by name; that `_handleWheel` is their only client-px caller | Pivot within 1.44 px in seven engines; bpmn-js 75 px per step at every zoom |
| 4 `ContextPad` `_getPosition`                           | Difference of two client rects written as container px                                 | Private member `_getTargetBounds` set on the instance                               | `_getTargetBounds` (Wardley's subclass overrides the same)       | Pad gap 4.4 / 6.4 / 8.8 / 13.2 / 17.5 px, as `overlay`                      |
| 5 `PopupMenu` `_ensureVisible` (`position: fixed`)      | Client px in a fixed box under a transformed ancestor; (60, 85) px off at zoom 1 too   | Private member `_ensureVisible` set on the instance, the popup's width pinned       | `_ensureVisible`                                                 | (0, 5) px for 8 of 8 placements up to zoom 1, 7 of 8 at 1.5, 0 of 8 at 2    |
| 6 `HoverTooltip` with `PaletteTooltip`                  | The same for the palette tooltip                                                       | Public `hoverTooltip.add()` wrapped                                                 | None; sources must register after the module                     | (8, 0) px at five zooms                                                     |
| 7 `BendpointUtil` `toCanvasCoordinates`                 | The `Dragging` formula in an imported function: nothing starts at 4 of 5 zooms         | Service override: `bendpoints`, `connectionSegmentMove` on a facade                 | None by name                                                     | Starts 5 of 5, within 0.97 and 0.68 px                                      |
| 8, 9 `DirectEditing` `complete`, `TextBox` `autoResize` | The text box's client rect compared by `!==` with container px: every close is a write | Public event `directEditing.activate` and an own `getBoundingClientRect` on the box | `$textbox`, `_active.context.bounds`                             | 0 of 36 closes write                                                        |
| Not repaired: `TextBox` resize handle                   | Client delta added to the box size in a closure                                        | Not reachable                                                                       |                                                                  | Box grows zoom times the pointer, the width is stored                       |
| Not repaired: `AutoScroll`                              | 20 client px thresholds against a scaled host                                          | Left alone (the builder judged it consistent)                                       |                                                                  | Right at 0.25 to 4, a drag is displaced at board zoom 0.1                   |

The numbers are those of `naive`: the module does not touch this site |

What a capability in the modeler contract would have to say if the renderers fixed their math at the
source (the plugin builder's seven rules; no value needs to be passed in):

1. Outer scale: the modeler is correct when its container is rendered under an ancestor CSS transform
   that is a uniform scale s > 0 plus a translation; s may change at any time, also during a gesture
   and while an editor or a popup is open.
2. The modeler derives s itself at each use (container client width over its layout width) and never
   caches it.
3. Pointer to diagram: `origin + (client - containerRect.origin) / (viewbox.scale * s)`, or the
   inverse screen matrix of the viewport group. Client deltas become container px by dividing by s.
4. Whatever is written as `left`, `top`, `width`, `height` inside the container comes from viewbox
   math alone, or from client rect differences divided by s. Client sizes are never used as layout
   sizes.
5. Nothing inside the container is `position: fixed` with client coordinates. A box that must leave
   the container goes to the top layer or to a host-provided layer, in a named coordinate space.
6. Constants meant in screen px (the 8 px gaps, the 22 px team band, clamp margins) are container px,
   and clamping uses the container's layout box.
7. Conformance: every pointer interaction of the suite runs at s = 0.5, 0.73, 1.5 and 2 inside a
   translated and scaled ancestor and must give the result of s = 1.

For diagram-js itself the equivalent is one canvas method (client point to local point, client delta
to container px) that the nine sites use instead of raw rect math.

### 5. Cost

Production build of the two spike pages, served locally with the Live Host's cache rule, one page at
a time, a fresh browser context per sample, BPMN editors on the stress board, 3 samples per cell.
Times are the medians of run 1. Memory rows give run 1 and run 2.

| Metric                                                              | naive        | counter      | overlay      | iframe       | plugin       | shim         |
| ------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------ | ------------ | ------------ | ------------ | ------------ | ------------ |
| M1 all editors rendered, 1 editor, cold (ms)                        | 538          | 526          | 529          | 565          | 518          | 528          |
| M1 the same, 10 editors (ms)                                        | 726          | 733          | 716          | 1117         | 721          | 724          |
| M1 the same, 30 editors (ms)                                        | 1171         | 1120         | 1179         | 2548         | 1109         | 1128         |
| M1 slope per further editor, 1 to 30 (ms)                           | 21.3         | 19.6         | 21.9         | 67.5         | 20.3         | 21.0         |
| M1 mixed board of 7 notations (ms)                                  | 744          | 764          | 763          | 961          | 782          | 757          |
| M2 JS heap, 10 editors (MB)                                         | 11.59, 11.59 | 11.60, 11.61 | 11.61, 11.64 | 20.49, 20.49 | 11.64, 11.64 | 11.62, 11.62 |
| M2 JS heap, 30 editors (MB)                                         | 20.18, 20.19 | 20.22, 20.22 | 20.22, 20.23 | 49.33, 49.33 | 20.25, 20.24 | 20.22, 20.22 |
| M2 JS heap, slope per further editor (KB)                           | 473, 472     | 473, 474     | 473, 473     | 1486, 1486   | 474, 474     | 473, 473     |
| M2 renderer anonymous resident memory, 30 editors (MB)              | 89.7, 89.8   | 89.9, 90.1   | 89.7, 90.1   | 216.6, 203.0 | 89.8, 89.5   | 89.5, 89.9   |

What holds in both runs: the heap ratio of `iframe` (3.1), a mount slope of about 3 times the in-page
one (run 2: 98.8 against 22.7 to 26.4 ms), the 1.5
to 1.9 ms of extra layout per wheel step in `counter` and `overlay` (20 viewbox writes per 20 steps
in every sample, 0 in `naive`, `plugin`, `shim`), no polling at rest, and no pointer move that differs
by more than about 0.8 ms between strategies. Controls: an empty frame costs 344 KB of heap, a frame
with the embed page and no editor 463 KB, the test tool's own world per frame 164 KB. Without the
tool a frame editor is about 1322 KB (derived by subtraction).

Caveats:

- Every time figure rests on run 1 alone: one run, 3 samples per cell. Run 2 was taken on a disturbed
  host (the virtual machine's host took 12.7 to 58 % of the processor time in 19 later probes; the
  same work cost 1.5 to 1.7 times the script time) and is no confirmation. A third run was abandoned.
  Of 1092 time cells 186 are flagged as disagreeing between the runs.
- `iframe`'s resident memory moved 6 to 10 % between the runs while its heap was identical: use the
  spans 199 to 224 MB at 30 editors and 5481 to 6300 KB per editor.
- Headless Chromium composites in software. Frame intervals, the main thread's paint share and the
  tenfold compositing cost of `iframe` in M6 are not those of a machine with a GPU. Only comparisons
  between strategies carry.
- The server is local: a cold load pays no transfer, and no load in a harness context uses V8's code
  cache.
- The build stage measured the frame on the dev server and got other absolute values (1.67 MB against
  0.59 MB of heap, 10.3 MB against 1.5 MB of renderer memory per editor, one run): unbundled modules.
  The heap ratio agrees (2.8 there, 3.1 here, 2.8 without the tool's world); the resident memory per
  editor does not.

### 6. The board as an artifact

The board file is a header and one JSON line per element, sorted by id, keys sorted, whole numbers.
Measured in 147 browser rows (two runs, all verdicts equal) and 28 pure rows.

| Topic                  | Finding                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| ---------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| File at rest           | Demo board: 12 elements, 2018 B in 13 lines. Load and serialise is byte-identical in the page and in Node. 182 B per element: 9089 B for 50 elements, 36301 B for 200.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| Cost per gesture       | 28 of 29 writing gestures change exactly the lines of the elements they touch, in one write, the rest byte-identical. A move is 1 line of 120 to 225 B, a new arrow 1 line of 123 B, a group move 1 line (members keep relative coordinates), deleting a group with four members 7 lines. Arm, disarm, pan, zoom, select: 0 lines, 0 writes. The exception: a group resized from its top left corner rewrites the group and all 4 members (5 lines, 881 B). A one character edit of a note shows as its whole line (224 B). |
| Images                 | The bytes are a file of their own (`assets/<name>`); the board file gains one line of 132 to 135 B for a 200 KiB and for a 2 MiB picture. Pasted pictures are named `image.png`, `image-2.png` against this client's files only, and a deleted image node leaves its file behind.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| Two peers              | 10 concurrent cases on two documents, both client-id orders: all converge to the same bytes, 4 leave wrong data. A node dropped into a group that the peer deletes keeps the dead parent and is drawn 1000, 0 off. An arrow to a node the peer deletes stays in the file, undrawn and unreachable. A drop into a group against a move gives one peer's group and the other's coordinates (1050, 500 for drops at 1300, 400 and 50, 500). A member moved while its group is resized from the top left stands 100, 100 off.   |
| Two peers, page        | A peer's move shows at once, a node held by the pointer does not jump, a node deleted under the pointer leaves no ghost, an armed node deleted by a peer leaves no editor behind (6 of 8 rows ok). A note editor opened and closed without typing writes the old text back over a peer's edit (wrong data); with typing the peer's sentence is replaced without a notice.                                                                                                                                                   |
| Git merge              | `git merge-file` conflicts where the live document does not: neighbouring lines (5 of 15 pairs; 15 of 15 clean with an empty line between elements, which today's writer drops), two attributes of one element, two new nodes in one gap (9, 15 and 23 of 120 trials on random ids, 120 of 120 on a board with hand-written ids). Two merges that Git calls clean are wrong: a dangling arrow and a dangling parent. `merge=union` loses a move. The reader drops a line that is no JSON without a word.                    |
| Actions                | The action layer writes the same lines as the pointer in 27 of 27 gestures that have a counterpart, when the caller passes what React Flow computes. `writeRemoval(store, [node], [])` called plainly leaves the node's arrow dangling. Pointer only: group resize, image file import, selection. Program only: undo (Control+Z does nothing on the board; `store.undo` restores a delete byte for byte). Nowhere: z order. The page hands a script neither the store nor the actions.                                      |
| Derived arrows         | 54 of 54 rows ok in `overlay`, `plugin`, `iframe`: the pairs equal what the platform's reader finds, they follow an edit made in the armed editor, by the engine and by a peer, cannot be selected or deleted, and are never in the board file.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| Beside an armed editor | 11 cases x `overlay`, `plugin`, `iframe` x zoom 0.5 and 1.5 (66 pages per run): no board gesture changed a model file or removed the armed node. 42 ok, 20 degraded, 4 broken (the two `iframe` gestures in section 3). In all three the armed node's four arrow handles take no press, and its left resize line keeps 2 to 3 of 40 px. Control+A and Delete typed while armed empty the model (35 of 35 elements; Control+Z restores them): the keys are the editor's.                                                     |

What the format would need before two branches change it: an element-wise 3-way merge (as a Git
merge driver and before a release pull request), a validator rule and a repair for dangling
references, duplicate ids and unparsable lines, coordinates that cannot be half-written (absolute
x, y with the parent as membership only, or the parent written with every drop), a note's text as a
text type, and image names that two branches cannot both pick. None of this was built.

### 7. Behaviour that every strategy shares

A product has to decide these whichever strategy it takes.

| Behaviour                                                         | Number                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| ----------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| A peer's edit closes an open bpmn-js or DRD label editor          | The typed text is lost without a notice in 3 of 3 rows per strategy (12 of 12 rows per run with four strategies in round 3). A decision table cell keeps its text and loses editor and focus. Wardley and Context Map keep both. An open popup closes in 5 of 5 rows, the selection goes in 4 of 5.                                                                                                                                               |
| Two simultaneous moves of different shapes                        | One is undone with a notice in 2 of 9 rows per strategy, depending on the file's history: BPMN once the peer's shape was moved before (its DI line is then 3 lines from the other's), DMN on the first write (65 lines re-serialised). Never silently, never twice. A held drag is protected in 9 of 9 rows.                                                                                                                                      |
| A label editor closed without typing writes at a fractional scale | At the node's fitted canvas scale (0.676) the editor asks for 67.6 x 54.08 px and reads back 67.594 x 54.078: an empty BPMNLabel element is added, the event label box goes from 742, 315, 72, 14 to 741, 315, 74, 14, the annotation grows 2.96 units (`shim`), 1.48 to 5.92 (`counter`, `overlay`), 2.68 (`iframe`). At every zoom, in every strategy but `plugin`. At editor zoom 100 % `counter` and `overlay` still write, at 0.73 and 1.37. |
| Focus after Escape                                                | After Escape closes a popup or a label editor the focus is on the document's body until diagram-js's debounced restore runs (80 of 80 measurements). A key pressed in that gap does not reach the editor: 3 of 20 rehearsal rows lost it. The gap's length was only measured under load.                                                                                                                                                          |
| Focus after a press on a non-focusable part of the armed editor   | The focus is on the body (in the frame: its own body). Keys reach neither the editor nor a board action until the canvas is clicked.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| Chrome that scales (`naive`, `iframe`, `plugin`, `shim`)          | bpmn-js pad entries 11 / 16.1 / 22 / 33 / 44 px at zoom 0.5 / 0.73 / 1 / 1.5 / 2, renderer pad entries 14 / 20 / 28 / 42 / 56 px, palette entries 16 / 23 / 32 / 48 / 64 px, table text 7 / 10.2 / 14 / 21 / 28 px. A 24 px target needs zoom 1 for the renderers and more than 1 for bpmn-js.                                                                                                                                                    |
| Chrome that keeps its size (`counter`, `overlay`)                 | Every pad, popup and palette row is ok from zoom 1 in an 800 x 508 node. Below it content is covered or lost (4 of 14 palette entries at 0.5), and at board zoom 0.25 no DRD or Context Map shape can be pressed.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| Sizes that are the engine's own                                   | The label editors of Value Chain (6.0 px text at 0.5), Team Topology (4.9 px at 0.5, 7.1 px at 0.73) and Context Maps (5.8 and 8.4 px) follow the diagram in all six strategies. Wardley's colour swatches are 22 px.                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| Popups at an edge                                                 | diagram-js flips a popup by its whole size with no clamp. `iframe`: 17 % of the replace menu cut in a 640 x 388 node. `shim` (builder): in the board's corner at zoom 2 the menu ends 156 px above the window, 9 of 15 entries in reach. `plugin`: cut by the board, 60 % and 47 % visible in the two measured cases. `counter`, `overlay`: the menu leaves the node by 28 to 100 %.                                                              |
| A click on the board's toolbar while editing                      | Commits and closes an open label editor in all six (30 of 30 rows), closes a popup in the five in-page strategies (20 of 20) and not in the frame (4 of 4).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| A node resized while armed                                        | The view is kept, so the palette no longer fits a 500 x 320 node: 10 of 14 bpmn entries inside the host (8 of 14 in `counter`, `overlay`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| FEEL cell                                                         | The first click into a cell puts the caret at the end (12 of 12 rows in round 3, four strategies); the second click is exact. The first focus logs `CodeMirror plugin crashed: TypeError: tags is not iterable`, also in the embed page alone.                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| A click on a column border of the decision table                  | A press and release without a move writes `biodi:width` for a column that had none, in every strategy (192 for a 192 wide column in `overlay`, `iframe`, `plugin`, `shim`, 3 of 3 rows each). The engine does that. Only `naive` writes a wrong value: 150 at zoom 0.5 and 288 at 1.5, and the column jumps on the screen (section 10).                                                                                                           |

### 8. Known bugs that remain, and what was not measured

Fixed in round 4 and no longer in the tables: the click stop on the armed node body, the focusable
node wrapper, `pointer-events: all` inherited into the editor, a drag released over the pane
disarming the node, the preview chrome that made the decision table jump 49 px.

Fixed in round 5 and no longer in the table below:

| Where                                                 | What was wrong                                                                                       | Fix                                                                                                                                                                                             | Measured                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| ----------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `board-arming.ts` (`ARMED_EDITOR`), `board.tsx`       | `overlay`: Backspace after a press on the armed editor's palette deleted another selected board node | A press in the overlay layer counts as a press in the armed editor (one selector)                                                                                                               | Builder's script only, the same steps before and after: the other node deleted before, both nodes remain after, also with the rules off. No battery row presses a delete key with another board node selected.                                  |
| `engines/view-controller.ts`, `engines/use-engine.ts` | `counter`, `overlay`: the editor's own zoom left diagram-js's range of 0.2 to 4 after a board zoom   | While the editor takes input, k times board zoom is kept inside 0.2 to 4; the lower end widens to the fitted k times zoom, so a view nobody zoomed is never changed; a preview is never clamped | wheel-limits: degraded in 6 of 6 rows before, ok in 10 of 10 after (builder: `counter` and `overlay`, a step in x1.118 to x1.183 where it was x2). `bpmn-chrome` control run on `overlay`: 5 of 5 ok, the only 5 rows that differ from round 4. |

Not measured for the clamp: an armed `counter` or `overlay` editor zoomed out to its own lower end
shows a picture larger than its preview once k times board zoom falls below 0.2.

Fixed after the batteries, with no effect on a measurement: three words in `sync-view-chromium.ts`,
`board-chromium.ts`, `lib.ts` and `engines/coordinate-shim/space.ts` were Tailwind utility names (a
negated variable in code at three places, the one-word spelling of anti-aliased in a result text, the
hyphenated CSS property name of a backdrop filter in a regular expression and in a diagnostic
string). With the spike in the tree they added three rules, 709 B, to each of the product's two style
sheets. Two variables are renamed and two strings and the expression reworded (11 lines); the mirror
build is byte-identical again (275 files). Whoever edits a file of the spike, this README included,
runs the mirror build again. Round 5 met it once more: one word in a test file, the hyphenated CSS
keyword for an easing that starts and ends slowly, added a rule; it is reworded, and the mirror
build after every stage of the round and on its final state is identical (275 files).

under a label editor that crosses the board's edge. Left from the first of the two: after Done by
the keyboard the focus falls to the body.

| Column width under `plugin`                 | Builder: stored 232 / 231.7 / 232 / 232 / 232 for a drag of 40 flow units                  | `dmn`: 272 / 246.79 / 232 / 218.67 / 212 for a drag of 40 screen px; both right within 1, neither whole at 0.73           |
| Wardley's evolve handle                     | Plugin builder: within 0.74 px in every strategy                                           | `miragon-a`: 0.3 to 1.1 px (`plugin`), 0.5 to 1.3 (`shim`), up to 2.2 px (`iframe` at zoom 2)                             |
| Where the no-change label commit happens    | Shim builder: bpmn-js, DRD, Context Maps, Value Chain                                      | `miragon-a`, `miragon-b`: not in Context Maps and Value Chain (15 of 15 rows byte-identical)                              |
| The shim's window-size gap                  | Builder: a cell in the right half of a 2200 px window at zoom 0.5 lies beyond `innerWidth` | `dmn` extra script: a pan cannot produce it; the cause is `clientWidth` and `clientHeight`, which the shim does not patch |
| What the fix changed in `overlay`, `iframe` | Fix stage: their rows are unchanged everywhere                                             | `bpmn-chrome`: 15 of their rows changed in their text (focus on the body, Tab order), none in its verdict                 |
| Memory of a frame                           | Build stage (dev server, one run): 10.3 MB against 1.5 MB of renderer memory per editor    | Cost run (production build): 5481 to 6300 KB against 1801 to 1857 KB                                                      |

Not measured, and it matters for the decision:

- Any browser but Chromium 158 headless. A touch device, pen, IME (a two-finger pinch was only
  emulated, section 12). A machine with a GPU, a headed browser. Device pixel ratio 2 beyond drags
  and sharpness.
- A second quiet cost run: no time figure is measured twice. Frames, arming, board work and idle at
  30 nodes. Memory after a long session. Cost of the `plugin` and `shim` variants.
- The combination of `plugin` for diagram views with a net-scale-1 surface or a frame for HTML views:
  not built. The CSS `zoom` property on an HTML view's container: not tried.
- Renderers with the seven rules implemented do not exist; `own` shows three kinds of site and was
  run once per battery. None of the suggested fixes of the plugin module and of the shim was tried,
  except the shim's client-size counter-test from the harness.
- `shim`: a real modal dialog or popover of an engine, the caller rule under real hand input, the FEEL
  gap in a natural setting (a node taller than the window, zoomed out to fit, is derived, not run).
- A minimum arm zoom, zoom-to-node on arming, a board zoom that disarms or is locked while a node
  is armed: built and measured in round 5, an exact arm zoom as the default in round 6. Sections
  12 and 13 list what is not measured for them, among it the board battery and the cost run, which
  were not repeated under the rules.
- A popup over a neighbouring node, at the board's bottom edge, of a node in the board's corner (only
  the shim builder's own case). A popup while the board zooms: bpmn-js, the DRD, Wardley and Context
  Map only.
- bpmn-js: message flows, associations, boundary events, drill-down, pads of connections and lanes,
  copy and paste between two nodes. dmn-js: the boxed expression view, lists by keyboard, a file
  without DMNDI. Renderers: the Keyboard shortcuts dialog in-page (unmeasured, 15 rows per strategy),
  Event Storming's spelling tooltip, Context Maps' legend tip, Wardley's pipelines.
- Two peers through a real room: the in-page hub relays synchronously, so its rows are the optimistic
  case. More than two peers, reconnect, an offline replica.
- The board: a merge on a hosting service, a merge driver, undo on the page beyond one case, more
  than 200 elements, the product's validator (no notation is registered for a board file), the
  armed cases in `naive`, `counter` and `shim`.
- The old four strategies ran once on the fixed board. React StrictMode, hot updates, a production
  build of the embed page for anything but cost.
